Quat)\n\t{\n\t\tfloat tr = Mat[0][0] + Mat[1][1] + Mat[2][2];\n\t\tif (tr > 0.0f)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tfloat InvS = rsqrt(tr + 1.f);\n\t\t\tfloat s = 0.5f * InvS;\n\n\t\t\tQuat.x = (Mat[1][2] - Mat[2][1]) * s;\n\t\t\tQuat.y = (Mat[2][0] - Mat[0][2]) * s;\n\t\t\tQuat.z = (Mat[0][1] - Mat[1][0]) * s;\n\t\t\tQuat.w = 0.5f * rcp(InvS);\n\t\t}\n\t\telse if ( (Mat[0][0] > Mat[1][1]) && (Mat[0][0] > Mat[2][2])
